# Seed 62 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - seed 60's second replicate: its small end where theirs is, its fast end 5% behind (Iteration 433)

`seed 62 tag2 11h widths noevolved then experiment footprint.py then experiment review-checks.py spread` at 017b7ca97
(Iteration 425's bundle - before 428-432, so without the Tegra's rebuild test and the head to head, which move to seed
63's chain): seed 60's setup a third time. The remote checks started 03:57:18, the evolution 04:03:14; it evolved
10 h 20 min. 38,837 designs, generations 0-155: 31,071 alive, 7,547 screened (20.0%), 219 refused at the reach limit.
Then footprint.py (34 minutes) and review-checks.py spread over seeds 58-62 (10 minutes).

**No verdict** - no comparison step; seed 63's places it. **Its ends, by its own measures:** fastest 0.116 (seeds 60
and 61: 0.111 and 0.110), smallest 15,897 bytes (15,930 and 15,907); at 32 bits its fastest 557c7e72ff 0.315 (0.325
and 0.344) - the best of the three there. **The replicates at their common generation, 133** (review-checks.py spread):
fastest 0.1111, 0.1108 and 0.1164 - seed 62 5.05% behind the other two; smallest 15,938, 15,939 and 15,929 bytes,
within 0.06%; hypervolume 0.4956, 0.4990 and 0.4965, within 0.7%; gene usage 7.5-11.8 points apart a pair. One basin at
the small end, and chance at the fast end: about 5% between runs of one start.

**Facts.** Its front: 192 designs, all tag-2; tos on 168, the JIT on 93, tail and msc on none. heldout (sieve): Spearman
0.973, the fastest third 0.728, the fastest tenth 0.463; the pick's regret +1.6%. Fast-end optimism: the fastest tenth
re-measured 1.066 of its in-run speed. Diversity 0.488 at the end; the smallest's last gain at generation 141 of 155,
the best speed's last 2% at 45. At 32 bits 1,966 failed, every one a known cause: 979 on the corpus, bfind without tos;
987 compiles, tail (clang with preserve_none 490, GCC 363, clang 134) - fixed by the after-seed-62 batch, applied at
this processing. **footprint.py** (HANDOFF item 2; 12 of the front, programs of 100 to 30,000 words): the selection's
order holds, weakening with size - Spearman 0.951, 0.930, 0.783, 0.783 - but each design's time per call against s6
moves 1.6-3.8 times across the sizes: the fast designs, about 4 times s6's speed at 100 and 1,000 words, keep only 1.1
to 1.5 times it at 10,000, before s6 falls off its own cliff at 30,000 (its time per call 154 to 464 ns). Its chain's
remote checks, of seed 61's front: results/xcheck-seed62.md.

---
# Evolved VM designs

31290 designs evaluated, 31071 alive, 7547 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): f30818656b, speed 0.136, binary + image 30,591, memory 160 KB - Q 0.204 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
